([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/sra-data-retrieval))
- **sra-data-retrieval-and-ingestion**: Use when your analysis requires raw sequencing reads stored in NCBI SRA (identified by SRR, SRX, or SRP accessions) OR you have local FASTQ files organized in a directory structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/sra-data-retrieval-and-ingestion))
- **sra-dataset-download-and-preprocessing**: Use when a de novo transcriptome-assembly benchmark must start from the paper's exact read sets — you have SRA accession lists (e.g. Human SRR5133163, Mouse SRX648736) or need the RSEM-simulated Human RNA-Seq data from Supplementary Section S3. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sra-dataset-download-and-preprocessing))
- **sra-dataset-retrieval**: Use when a plant tncRNA or small-RNA analysis needs raw single-end sRNA-seq data from public archives, and the run must be downloaded from NCBI SRA as FASTQ before adapter/quality trimming. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sra-dataset-retrieval--97f4a900cecc72ed))
- **sra-dataset-retrieval**: Use when a benchmark or reproduction requires the exact real RNA-Seq reads used in the rnaSPAdes/GigaScience 2019 paper, identified by the paper's SRA accessions (e.g., Human SRR5133163, Human large SRR1957703/SRR1957706). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sra-dataset-retrieval--71efed9322d97840))
- **sra-fastq-data-retrieval**: Use when an analysis pipeline starts from a public NCBI SRA run accession (e.g. SRR6287056) and raw paired-end FASTQ files for both mates must be downloaded before preprocessing steps such as hg19 host-read depletion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sra-fastq-data-retrieval))
- **sra-fastq-data-retrieval**: Use when raw RNA-seq data for a study must be fetched from NCBI SRA via SRR/ERR run accessions or a study accession (SRP) before quantification with tools like Seq2Fun 2.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sra-fastq-data-retrieval))
- **sra-fastq-retrieval**: Use when reproducing a published RNA-seq analysis and raw reads must be fetched from SRA before trimming, alignment (STAR), and quantification (RSEM/DESeq2); triggered when a supplemental accession table lists the samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sra-fastq-retrieval))
- **sra-long-read-data-retrieval**: Use when a benchmark or reproduction requires empirical long-read WGS read sets paired with specific reference assemblies — e.g. NCBI SRA accessions (SRR10028111, SRR13070625) or GIAB HG002 PacBio HiFi data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sra-long-read-data-retrieval))
- **sra-metagenome-data-acquisition**: Use when a cohort's raw shotgun metagenomic or metatranscriptomic reads must be retrieved from NCBI SRA via a BioProject accession list (e.g. PRJNA389280) before mapping reads to gene clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/sra-metagenome-data-acquisition))
- **sra-metagenomic-data-retrieval**: Use when a downstream variant-discovery or strain-analysis pipeline (e.g. cellsnp-lite + MetaQuad) needs raw sequencing reads from a publicly archived metagenomic study and only the BioProject accession is known. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sra-metagenomic-data-retrieval))
- **sra-read-data-retrieval**: Use when a benchmark or reproduction task requires downloading raw short-read and long-read FASTQ data for specific samples identified by archive accessions (e.g. BioProject PRJDB4377, Supplementary Table S7). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sra-read-data-retrieval--1e24d946195561c2))
- **sra-read-data-retrieval**: Use when a task requires reproducing the MTBC_B (n=1,609) Mykrobe resistance-prediction validation and the raw isolate FASTQ reads must first be downloaded from ENA/NCBI SRA using accession codes in Supplementary Data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sra-read-data-retrieval--1b1baecb6ccc2b1b))
- **sra-read-retrieval**: Use when a benchmark or tutorial pipeline requires the raw reads for a specific SRA accession (e.g. SRR961514 Mock B viral community) as FASTQ input for downstream trimming and metagenomic assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sra-read-retrieval--4040e3d73da7afd3))
- **sra-read-retrieval**: Use when a task provides an SRA accession (e.g. SRR9875293, SRR3458562) or BioProject (PRJNA431646, PRJNA319556, PRJNA395784) and local FASTQ input is needed for downstream VirMAP or metagenomic classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sra-read-retrieval--68750dac97b9de25))
- **sra-read-retrieval**: Use when a benchmark workflow specifies an SRA accession (e.g. SRX6602475) as the real biological read input and the raw nanopore FASTQ/FASTA reads must be downloaded before classification or alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/sra-read-retrieval--c180b3dc28f6fe52))
- **sra-rnaseq-data-retrieval**: Use when reproducing the UMI mapping-shift analysis or Table 1 cluster-size read fractions and raw FASTQ files for any of run_171108, run_170420, SCRB, La Manno, Yanai1, or Yanai2 are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/sra-rnaseq-data-retrieval))
- **sra-wgs-fastq-retrieval**: Use when the analysis goal is to benchmark or reproduce variant counts on public WGS/WES data identified by SRA run or BioProject accessions (e.g. SRR12474733, PRJNA657938) and the raw FASTQ files must be downloaded from NCBI SRA first. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/sra-wgs-fastq-retrieval))

- **sragent**: Query the Sequence Read Archive (SRA), retrieve scientific publications, and analyze genomics metadata using the SRAgent toolkit. Supports accession conversion (GSE→SRX→SRR), BigQuery metadata queries, manuscript downloads from multiple sources, and scRNA-seq technology identification. Use when working with SRA/GEO datasets, finding publications, or analyzing single-cell sequencing experiments. ([aiskillstore/marketplace](https://github.com/aiskillstore/marketplace/tree/HEAD/skills/arcinstitute/sragent) · ★487 · 7 copies)
- **sram-cim-snn-accelerator**: SRAM-Based Compute-in-Memory Accelerator for Linear-decay Spiking Neural Networks. Leverages in-memory computing to eliminate the von Neumann bottleneck for SNN inference using linear-decay neuron models compatible with CIM crossbar arrays for ultra-low power edge inference. Activation: SRAM CIM SNN, compute-in-memory spiking network, linear-decay SNN accelerator, CIM crossbar SNN, in-memory computing spiking neural network. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/sram-cim-snn-accelerator) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **sram-mapping**: Analyze Verilog RTL to identify SRAM macro instances and determine their mapping to sky130hd process SRAM macros or standard-cell alternatives. ([xsw632/ChipAgent](https://github.com/xsw632/ChipAgent/tree/HEAD/skills/sram-mapping) · ★8)
- **sram-oidc**: Add SURF SRAM login (OIDC via the SRAM proxy) to a CEDA app — the authlib OAuth2 flow, the OIDC_* config, and the auth-disabled-by-default fallback used in the CEDA Streamlit apps. Use when wiring SRAM authentication, editing OIDC config, or debugging an SRAM login flow. ([cedanl/.github](https://github.com/cedanl/.github/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sram-oidc))
- **srbench-eval**: Evaluates sequential recommendation models across accuracy, fairness, stability, and efficiency dimensions. It tests whether models can correctly rank items based on user interaction history and assesses their robustness, bias, and computational cost. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Yelp, ML-100K, Beauty, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Recall@5. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/srbench-eval) · ★3)
